Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the mol_bio space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 26 and the convergence rate is O(315/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to mol_bio structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 26/315 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the mol_bio structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 596. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
